<script async="" type="text/javascript">
function getFHCookie(cname) {
    var name = cname + "=";
    var decodedCookie = decodeURIComponent(document.cookie);
    var ca = decodedCookie.split(';');
    for(var i = 0; i <ca.length; i++) {
        var c = ca[i];
        while (c.charAt(0) == ' ') {
            c = c.substring(1);
        }
        if (c.indexOf(name) == 0) {
            return c.substring(name.length, c.length);
        }
    }
    return "";
}

<div class="col-md-6" data-click-count="0">
<h6 data-click-count="0">What is Mutual Fund?</h6>
<div class="content" data-click-count="0">
<p data-click-count="0">For first timers, mutual funds may sound complex, but in reality mutual funds are anything but intimidating. Mutual funds are in fact for the benefit of those who want to take the advantage of investing across equity, debt, gold, etc, but may not have the skill set, time or financial wherewithal to do so. This is exactly where a mutual fund comes in as it is a professionally managed collective investment vehicle that lets you invest in small amounts and reap big benefits, so long as you choose the right schemes in line with the goals you have set for yourself. Mutual funds also provide you multiple advantages of flexibility, liquidity, tax benefits and diversification of risk at a very small price. In this video we decode mutual funds for you.</p>
</div>
<a class="btn btn-default panel-orange-btn analytics-link" data-action="Learn and Grow" data-category="Homepage" data-click-count="0" data-event-name="Event Homepage" data-label="Know More | What is Mutual Fund" href="/investor/investor-education/video/what-is-a-mutual-fund-io04og32" id="know-more">Know More</a> <!-- <button class="btn panel-blue-btn submit"></button> --></div>

<div class="mediaItem thumbnailVid" data-click-count="0" title="Difference between Savings Vs Investing"><a class="analytics-link" data-action="Learn and Grow" data-category="Homepage" data-click-count="0" data-event-name="Event Homepage" data-label="Video | Difference between Savings Vs Investing" data-video-id="qZgNPwKIlHA" href="#"><img alt="Difference between Savings Vs Investing" class="thumbnail img-responsive" data-click-count="0" src="/content-international/images/en-in-retail/Learn-grow-Difference-between-saving-and-investing.png" title="Difference between Savings Vs Investing"/> <span class="icon icon-fti_play" data-click-count="0"></span> <span class="gridCaption green-bg" data-click-count="0">Difference between Saving...</span> </a>
<div class="content-block" data-click-count="0">
<div class="title" data-click-count="0">Difference between Savings Vs Investing</div>
<div class="item-content" data-click-count="0" data-redirectlink="/investor/investor-education/video/difference-between-saving-and-investing-io04og31" target="_blank">
<p data-click-count="0">A hard working individual who is earning a decent income in today’s date, is judicious about his expenses and even manages to put away 20% of his income. He may be content with himself because he is saving money, but he will be none the richer by his retirement age if he lets this money lie idle without earning any returns. To meet specific goals like children’s education, marriage or ensuring a comfortable retirement, one needs to put this money saved to good use over a period of time in a patient and disciplined manner. The process of doing so, is called investing. Through several examples we take a closer look at the fundamental difference between saving and investing and why you must focus on investing rather than let your money sit idle.</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="col-xs-6 col-sm-4 col-md-3 cloneditem-1" data-click-count="0">
<div class="mediaItem thumbnailVid" data-click-count="0" title="Benefits of Mutual Funds"><a class="analytics-link" data-action="Learn and Grow" data-category="Homepage" data-click-count="0" data-event-name="Event Homepage" data-label="Video | Benefits of Mutual Funds" data-video-id="_HbDCNDF9Cw" href="#"><img alt="Benefits of Mutual Funds" class="thumbnail img-responsive" data-click-count="0" src="/content-international/images/en-in-retail/Learn-grow-Benefits-of-mutual-funds.png" title="Benefits of Mutual Funds"/> <span class="icon icon-fti_play" data-click-count="0"></span> <span class="gridCaption green-bg" data-click-count="0">Benefits of Mutual Funds</span> </a>
<div class="content-block" data-click-count="0">
<div class="title" data-click-count="0">Benefits of Mutual Funds</div>
<div class="item-content" data-click-count="0" data-redirectlink="/investor/investor-education/video/benefits-of-investing-in-mutual-funds-io04og32" target="_blank">
<p data-click-count="0">Mutual funds are often misunderstood as a complex investment vehicle. In reality, however they are pretty simple in their investment philosophy and offer the investor a host of benefits such as diversification, professional money management, economies of scale, transparency, liquidity to mention a few. In this video we take you through the different benefits a mutual fund investor can avail of to grow his corpus and meet his financial goals by choosing the right kind of funds for the time horizon he has set to meet his financial goals.</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div><div class="col-xs-6 col-sm-4 col-md-3 cloneditem-2" data-click-count="0">
<div class="mediaItem thumbnailVid" data-click-count="0" title="Understanding Asset Classes"><a class="analytics-link" data-action="Learn and Grow" data-category="Homepage" data-click-count="0" data-event-name="Event Homepage" data-label="Video | Understanding Asset Classes" data-video-id="8g8gPAqFxmM" href="#"><img alt="Understanding Asset Classes" class="thumbnail img-responsive" data-click-count="0" src="/content-international/images/en-in-retail/Learn-grow-Understanding-asset-classes.png" title="Understanding Asset Classes"/> <span class="icon icon-fti_play" data-click-count="0"></span> <span class="gridCaption green-bg" data-click-count="0">Understanding Asset Class...</span> </a>
<div class="content-block" data-click-count="0">
<div class="title" data-click-count="0">Understanding Asset Classes</div>
<div class="item-content" data-click-count="0" data-redirectlink="/investor/investor-education/video/understanding-asset-classes-io04og31" target="_blank">
<p data-click-count="0">In order to invest your savings so that it earns the highest possible returns at the lowest possible risk, you need to choose various investment avenues each having unique characteristics of risk and returns. These are known as asset classes and examples include - cash, bonds, equities, real estate, gold and other commodities. Mutual funds offer you the benefit of investing in various asset classes and diversifying to reduce the risk of your investments. In this video we take you through all that you need to know about asset classes and tell you how you need a combination of these asset classes to meet you financial goals.</p>
</div>
</div>

<div class="content-block" data-click-count="0">
<div class="title" data-click-count="0">Consequences of Delay in Investing</div>
<div class="item-content" data-click-count="0" data-redirectlink="/investor/investor-education/video/the-consequence-of-delay-in-investing-io04og31" target="_blank">
<p data-click-count="0">Most young people tend to think that they have “enough time to invest in the future” and delay their investment process. But what they do not realise is that by “waiting for the right time” or out of sheer lethargy they are missing out on the power of compounding. In this video we explain why beginning your investment process early and staying invested over a long period of time will be more beneficial than investing a larger sum at a later date. Thus investing smaller sums of money systematically, regularly and patiently over a period of time will help you reach your financial goals slowly but steadily. Take a closer look to understand why a delay in investing may cost you dearly.</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div></div>
<div class="item" data-click-count="0">
<div class="col-xs-6 col-sm-4 col-md-3" data-click-count="0">
<div class="mediaItem thumbnailVid" data-click-count="0" title="Benefits of Mutual Funds"><a class="analytics-link" data-action="Learn and Grow" data-category="Homepage" data-click-count="0" data-event-name="Event Homepage" data-label="Video | Benefits of Mutual Funds" data-video-id="_HbDCNDF9Cw" href="#"><img alt="Benefits of Mutual Funds" class="thumbnail img-responsive" data-click-count="0" src="/content-international/images/en-in-retail/Learn-grow-Benefits-of-mutual-funds.png" title="Benefits of Mutual Funds"/> <span class="icon icon-fti_play" data-click-count="0"></span> <span class="gridCaption green-bg" data-click-count="0">Benefits of Mutual Funds</span> </a>
<div class="content-block" data-click-count="0">
<div class="title" data-click-count="0">Benefits of Mutual Funds</div>
<div class="item-content" data-click-count="0" data-redirectlink="/investor/investor-education/video/benefits-of-investing-in-mutual-funds-io04og32" target="_blank">
<p data-click-count="0">Mutual funds are often misunderstood as a complex investment vehicle. In reality, however they are pretty simple in their investment philosophy and offer the investor a host of benefits such as diversification, professional money management, economies of scale, transparency, liquidity to mention a few. In this video we take you through the different benefits a mutual fund investor can avail of to grow his corpus and meet his financial goals by choosing the right kind of funds for the time horizon he has set to meet his financial goals.</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="col-xs-6 col-sm-4 col-md-3 cloneditem-1" data-click-count="0">
<div class="mediaItem thumbnailVid" data-click-count="0" title="Understanding Asset Classes"><a class="analytics-link" data-action="Learn and Grow" data-category="Homepage" data-click-count="0" data-event-name="Event Homepage" data-label="Video | Understanding Asset Classes" data-video-id="8g8gPAqFxmM" href="#"><img alt="Understanding Asset Classes" class="thumbnail img-responsive" data-click-count="0" src="/content-international/images/en-in-retail/Learn-grow-Understanding-asset-classes.png" title="Understanding Asset Classes"/> <span class="icon icon-fti_play" data-click-count="0"></span> <span class="gridCaption green-bg" data-click-count="0">Understanding Asset Class...</span> </a>
<div class="content-block" data-click-count="0">
<div class="title" data-click-count="0">Understanding Asset Classes</div>
<div class="item-content" data-click-count="0" data-redirectlink="/investor/investor-education/video/understanding-asset-classes-io04og31" target="_blank">
<p data-click-count="0">In order to invest your savings so that it earns the highest possible returns at the lowest possible risk, you need to choose various investment avenues each having unique characteristics of risk and returns. These are known as asset classes and examples include - cash, bonds, equities, real estate, gold and other commodities. Mutual funds offer you the benefit of investing in various asset classes and diversifying to reduce the risk of your investments. In this video we take you through all that you need to know about asset classes and tell you how you need a combination of these asset classes to meet you financial goals.</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class="mediaItem thumbnailVid" data-click-count="0" title="What is Mutual Fund?"><a class="analytics-link" data-action="Learn and Grow" data-category="Homepage" data-click-count="0" data-event-name="Event Homepage" data-label="Video | What is Mutual Fund?" data-video-id="JduPJAf8QdY" href="#"><img alt="What is Mutual Fund?" class="thumbnail img-responsive" data-click-count="0" src="/content-international/images/en-in-retail/Learn-grow-What-is-a-mutual-fund.png" title="What is Mutual Fund?"/> <span class="icon icon-fti_play" data-click-count="0"></span> <span class="gridCaption green-bg" data-click-count="0">What is Mutual Fund?</span> </a>
<div class="content-block" data-click-count="0">
<div class="title" data-click-count="0">What is Mutual Fund?</div>
<div class="item-content" data-click-count="0" data-redirectlink="/investor/investor-education/video/what-is-a-mutual-fund-io04og32" target="_blank">
<p data-click-count="0">For first timers, mutual funds may sound complex, but in reality mutual funds are anything but intimidating. Mutual funds are in fact for the benefit of those who want to take the advantage of investing across equity, debt, gold, etc, but may not have the skill set, time or financial wherewithal to do so. This is exactly where a mutual fund comes in as it is a professionally managed collective investment vehicle that lets you invest in small amounts and reap big benefits, so long as you choose the right schemes in line with the goals you have set for yourself. Mutual funds also provide you multiple advantages of flexibility, liquidity, tax benefits and diversification of risk at a very small price. In this video we decode mutual funds for you.</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div></div>

<div class="title" data-click-count="0">What is Mutual Fund?</div>
<div class="item-content" data-click-count="0" data-redirectlink="/investor/investor-education/video/what-is-a-mutual-fund-io04og32" target="_blank">
<p data-click-count="0">For first timers, mutual funds may sound complex, but in reality mutual funds are anything but intimidating. Mutual funds are in fact for the benefit of those who want to take the advantage of investing across equity, debt, gold, etc, but may not have the skill set, time or financial wherewithal to do so. <div class="content-block" data-click-count="0">
<div class="title" data-click-count="0">Difference between Savings Vs Investing</div>
<div class="item-content" data-click-count="0" data-redirectlink="/investor/investor-education/video/difference-between-saving-and-investing-io04og31" target="_blank">
<p data-click-count="0">A hard working individual who is earning a decent income in today’s date, is judicious about his expenses and even manages to put away 20% of his income. He may be content with himself because he is saving money, but he will be none the richer by his retirement age if he lets this money lie idle without earning any returns. To meet specific goals like children’s education, marriage or ensuring a comfortable retirement, one needs to put this money saved to good use over a period of time in a patient and disciplined manner. The process of doing so, is called investing. Through several examples we take a closer look at the fundamental difference between saving and investing and why you must focus on investing rather than let your money sit idle.</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div></div>

<div class="title" data-click-count="0">Benefits of Mutual Funds</div>

<p data-click-count="0">Mutual funds are often misunderstood as a complex investment vehicle. In reality, however they are pretty simple in their investment philosophy and offer the investor a host of benefits such as diversification, professional money management, economies of scale, transparency, liquidity to mention a few. In this video we take you through the different benefits a mutual fund investor can avail of to grow his corpus and meet his financial goals by choosing the right kind of funds for the time horizon he has set to meet his financial goals.</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div><div class="col-xs-6 col-sm-4 col-md-3 cloneditem-3" data-click-count="0">
<div class="mediaItem thumbnailVid" data-click-count="0" title="Understanding Asset Classes"><a class="analytics-link" data-action="Learn and Grow" data-category="Homepage" data-click-count="0" data-event-name="Event Homepage" data-label="Video | Understanding Asset Classes" data-video-id="8g8gPAqFxmM" href="#"><img alt="Understanding Asset Classes" class="thumbnail img-responsive" data-click-count="0" src="/content-international/images/en-in-retail/Learn-grow-Understanding-asset-classes.png" title="Understanding Asset Classes"/> <span class="icon icon-fti_play" data-click-count="0"></span> <span class="gridCaption green-bg" data-click-count="0">Understanding Asset Class...</span> </a>
<div class="content-block" data-click-count="0">
<div class="title" data-click-count="0">Understanding Asset Classes</div>
<div class="item-content" data-click-count="0" data-redirectlink="/investor/investor-education/video/understanding-asset-classes-io04og31" target="_blank">
<p data-click-count="0">In order to invest your savings so that it earns the highest possible returns at the lowest possible risk, you need to choose various investment avenues each having unique characteristics of risk and returns. These are known as asset classes and examples include - cash, bonds, equities, real estate, gold and other commodities. Mutual funds offer you the benefit of investing in various asset classes and diversifying to reduce the risk of your investments. In this video we take you through all that you need to know about asset classes and tell you how you need a combination of these asset classes to meet you financial goals.</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div></div>
